([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-typeerror-transformstream-defa-4a2b3d012b94ca6c))
- **workerd-uncaught-exception-fetch-handl-896c63771fc6a69d**: Fixes the workerd crash when a fetch handler returns undefined or a non-Response value. Use when the worker throws instead of responding on certain code paths. Not for Response constructor errors, unhandled promise rejections, or handlers that return the wrong status. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-uncaught-exception-fetch-handl-896c63771fc6a69d))
- **workerd-uncaught-exception-in-schedule-bb3a4c99e7c92662**: Fixes cron triggers that look dead because the scheduled handler throws. This skill shows how to wrap the handler in try/catch with logging, verify the cron pattern is registered in wrangler.toml, and watch wrangler tail at the scheduled minute. Use it when a Worker cron never seems to run or silently does nothing. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-uncaught-exception-in-schedule-bb3a4c99e7c92662))
- **workerd-uncaught-exception-typeerror-f-8a617f614544d80c**: Diagnoses workerd's uncaught 'TypeError: fetch failed' from a worker. Use it when an outbound fetch blows up with no useful detail. The key trigger is the bare fetch failed TypeError; the fix is wrapping the call, logging the URL, and checking DNS, TLS, and reachability. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-uncaught-exception-typeerror-f-8a617f614544d80c))
- **workerd-uncaught-exception-unhandled-p-63b770d70f83d518**: Fixes unhandled promise rejections crashing the workerd fetch handler. Use it when tail shows an uncaught rejection with no await in sight. The key trigger is the unhandled rejection error; the fix is awaiting or catching every floating promise and moving background work to waitUntil. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-uncaught-exception-unhandled-p-63b770d70f83d518))
- **workerd-upstream-socket-hang-up-on-slo-ba5082ec6ef112c4**: Fixes workerd "upstream socket hang up" errors when a fetch to a slow origin dies mid-request. Use it when a worker proxying or calling an origin sees intermittent fetch failures that correlate with slow origin responses. Key trigger: the origin takes seconds to respond and the socket drops, so the fix is bounded retries with backoff plus a client-side timeout, and then fixing the origin's slowness. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-upstream-socket-hang-up-on-slo-ba5082ec6ef112c4))
- **workerd-websocket-connection-failed-be-34ed8406b6adac12**: Fixes workerd WebSocket connections failing before the handshake completes. Use it when a worker-side or proxied WebSocket upgrade never finishes. The key trigger is the pre-handshake failure; the fix is verifying the server returns a 101 with the right upgrade headers on the right route. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/workerd-websocket-connection-failed-be-34ed8406b6adac12))
